    <td class='blf'>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;The present line list includes only the strongest lines previously included in Reader&nbsp;et&nbsp;al.&nbsp;1980. Observed relative intensities are from Meggers&nbsp;et&nbsp;al.&nbsp;1975, except for one line at 6051.875&nbsp;&Aring; from Albertson&nbsp;et&nbsp;al., which was not included in Meggers&nbsp;et&nbsp;al. Its intensity reported by Albertson&nbsp;et&nbsp;al. was multiplied by ten to bring it into rough agreement with the intensity scale of Meggers&nbsp;et&nbsp;al. Energy-level classifications are mostly from Meggers&nbsp;et&nbsp;al., but some were added or revised based on close agreement with Ritz wavelengths of dipole-allowed transitions between known levels. The present list was prepared by A.&nbsp;Kramida (unpublished, 2016) for the release of ASD v.5.4.
